# FORM 1 DOSSIER: public/crm/SALE-FORM-REDESIGN.html (Sale listing form, "Sale Listing Form Redesign")

Owner: form1-owner. Written 2026-10-15. Repo is read-only for this work (working tree: branch `docs/owner-clarifications-2026-10-15`, HEAD `1b21cb4184eab1fe7fd698dfabaaa29412517d4c`; `git diff --stat tmp-audit-main HEAD -- public/crm app lib` is empty, so the code under test is identical to main `71381e1c` apart from a Master Plan doc). Server under test: http://localhost:3100, throwaway Postgres 127.0.0.1:55432/t. Nothing here was inferred from memory, old CSVs, rls-rules.json or earlier reports: every claim below was observed by driving the real page with Playwright, reading Postgres with psql, or reading the live Cotality API. Items I could not test are marked NOT TESTED.

How to use this document: section 0 = verdict and the ordered fix list; sections 1-6 = the specification the fixer needs; section 7 = the runnable acceptance script (`form1-acceptance.js`); section 8 = line-by-line registers (all 831 controls, all 159 script functions); section 9 = evidence index.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

## 0. VERDICT, COUNTS, ORDERED FIX LIST

**The form does not work end to end for its main purpose.** A Cotality-eligible sale (a normal condo/co-op sale) cannot be created from the UI: `POST /api/crm/listings` answers 422 from the legacy REBNY-CSV validator, and the toast shows only "Save failed: Request failed: 422". Website-only listings (listing type InHouseWebOnly / InHouseInternal) can be created, edited, published and viewed publicly, but reopen + save silently destroys saved data. Photo/floor-plan upload returned 404 on this server; the document input is dead.

Counts (computed from the live DOM, 831 controls = `input,select,textarea` on the page; tallies in section 8.1):

| measure | count |
|---|---|
| user-visible controls reviewed | 831 (438 checkbox, 105 radio, 102 number, 72 text, 41 select, 15 date, 13 textarea, 8 url, 5 email, 4 tel, 4 file, 3 datetime-local, 2 time, 19 hidden) |
| CORRECT (right Cotality field or right Mallan-only handling, verified) | 133 |
| REMAP (control is needed but wrong key/field/values/storage/restore) | 396 |
| DELETE (not sale-appropriate, dead, duplicate, or no valid target) | 49 |
| MALLAN-ONLY (private/commercial/profile data that must never be sent to Cotality) | 253 |
| controls whose value is NEVER saved (no id/name, hidden outside container, or not collected) | 48 |
| picklist option rows checked against live Cotality enums | 244 (keep 210, remap 21, no match 13) |
| script functions (159); dead code never referenced | 159 / 15 |

Counting rule: a group of checkboxes/radios sharing one `name` is one data item but every visible option is one row and carries the group verdict; the option-level keep/remap/delete is in the "verdict" column and in section 4.

### Ordered fix list (do in this order; each line = one acceptance check in section 7)

1. **Unblock creation of Cotality-eligible sales.** `lib/compliance/rebny-validator.ts` (line 128, rules from `lib/compliance/rls-rules.json`) and `lib/compliance/rls-enforcement.ts` demand fields that do not exist in live Cotality `Property` (live `$select` returns 400 for each): BuildingTaxLot, ElevatorsTotal, IDXEntireListingDisplayYN, NewDevelopmentYN, SyndicateYN, UnParsedAddress, PercentOfCommonElements, TaxMonthlyAmount, MaximumFinancingPercent, MaximumFinancingRemarks. It also demands fields a *new Mallan listing cannot have yet*: BuyerAgentMlsId, OriginalEntryTimestamp, SourceSystemKey, StandardStatus (draft), ListAgentMlsId (form never sends it, item 2). Replace the required list by the Cotality-validated list (section 2). Show the server reason in the toast (form `manualSaveDraft` line 8330 prints `err.message` = "Request failed: 422").
2. **Agent block.** Hidden `saleUpdatingAgent*` inputs are outside the `.flex-1` container read by `collectSaleFormData` (line 7401), so `ListAgentMlsId`, `ListAgentFullName`, `ListAgentEmail`, `ListAgentDirectPhone` are always "" (DB `list_agent_mls_id` empty for an agent whose `trestle_mls_id` is 148848). Fix: read by `getElementById`, or stamp from the session server-side; add `ListAgentKey` (= Member.MemberKey) distinct from `ListAgentMlsId` (= Member.MemberMlsId).
3. **Stop reopen/edit/save from destroying data.** On reopen these saved values do not come back, and the next Save writes them blank (verified by psql diff): Living Area (also blocks Submit with "1 required REBNY field missing"), Year Built, Half Baths, Total Baths, First Showing/ActivationDate, Admin Comments, Original Rooms, Tax Monthly, subway lines/station, and all 24 `commSale*` commission-request controls; `list_agent_mls_id` is blanked on PATCH; `saleLivingArea` value lands in `saleUnitSqFt`. Add the missing entries to `SALE_FIELD_MAP` (line 9173) and delete the duplicate area inputs.
4. **One status vocabulary = Cotality `StandardStatus`** (section 5): the form (26 options), `CRM_TO_RESO_STATUS` (line 8576) and the server state machine (`app/api/crm/listings/[id]/status/route.ts:26`) are three different vocabularies; Sold/Cancelled are not Cotality values; Closed/Canceled/Incomplete are rejected by the server (422) or silently saved as Active. Draft must be Mallan-only and must not be publicly viewable (a Draft page returned HTTP 200, robots `index, follow`).
5. **Opt-out flags must persist.** With AVM-display = No and Consumer-comment unchecked the payload carries `false` but the typed columns `internet_automated_valuation_display_yn` and `internet_consumer_comment_yn` stay `true` (verified). Also `expiration_date` stays NULL and `bathrooms_half` stays NULL for 0.
6. **Remove phantom Cotality keys from the payload** (live 400): BathroomsTotal, CoBrokeAgreement, BuildingPetsAllowed, AttendanceType, BuildingLaundryFeatures, FlipTax, SponsorUnitYN, TaxAbatementYN, TaxAbatementComments (+ auction_*, which are Mallan-only). Emit valid ones instead: BathroomsTotalInteger, PetsAllowed, LaundryFeatures, LivingAreaUnits, ConcessionsAmount, LandLeaseYN, StartShowingDate.
7. **Media/tour controls** (section 3): retarget Video URL / Video Tour URL / Branded Tour URL / Unbranded Tour 1-3 to `VirtualTourURLUnbranded|Branded[2|3]`; delete Photo Sort Order (mapped to a timestamp); fix or remove the Document input; get `/media/upload` mounted (404 here) and maintain PhotosCount/FloorPlansCount; use the shared media contract when published.
8. **Picklists** (section 4): fix the 45 option values that do not match live enums exactly (24 have a clear target member, 21 have none) (listing type, view, condition, pets, structure type, docs, laundry, dev status, levels).
9. **Keep private data private**: commission-request fields (seller/buyer names, buyer email/phone, attorneys, splits) and Building Profile contacts (super/manager/board emails) are written into the listing row `raw_data`; the building lookup (`/api/buildings/search`) returned `source:"db"` (built from CRM draft rows, with junk such as fee frequency "Weekly") instead of live Cotality.
10. **Access**: the agent form renders for a client/buyer session (`audit-buyer-token` shows "Audit Buyer" and a fully usable form; writes then 403); third-party (source-owned) listings open fully editable with no banner and Save fails with "Access denied"; the static `.html` is served to any cookie (including none) with 200.
11. **Compliance scanner false positives**: UCBA public-description rules are applied to Broker Comments (private remarks): "Seller is flexible" and "Call listing agent" block Submit.
12. **Agent/company pickers**: the listing/buyer/co-list pickers contain only "Mallan Real Estate Inc." and an empty agent list (`rebnyAgents = {mallan: []}`, line 5970); co-list agents cannot be chosen. Wire to the Mallan roster and the live Cotality `Member`/`Office` resources (section 6).
13. Dead code and dead controls: delete the 15 unreferenced functions, `saleDocInput`, `salePhotoSortOrder`, the 29 controls without id/name (or give them ids), `_offerDraftRestore` is never called so the unsaved-draft restore banner never appears.

## 1. PURPOSE AND DATA FLOW

**Purpose.** The CRM form where a Mallan broker/agent authors a Mallan-exclusive SALE listing (condo, co-op, condop, townhouse, house, etc.): address and building, status, price and fees, description, agents, open houses, features, building profile, distribution, media, commission request. It is not a viewer for third-party/IDX listings. Form 2 is the rental twin.

**Layout.** Seven main tabs `saleMainTab1..7` (1 Essentials with sub-tabs 1.1 Details, 1.2 Description, 1.3 Contacts, 1.4 Open houses; 2 Features with sub-tabs 2.1-2.3; 3 Townhouse/Building detail 3.1-3.3; 4 Distribution; 5 Preview (CRM summary / Public page preview); 6 History; 7 Commission request) plus two modals: Building Information (`saleBuildingModal`, 4 sub-tabs) and Media & Documents (`saleMediaModal`). Header buttons: Save Draft (`manualSaveDraft`), Submit (`submitSalesListing`).

**Create (new listing).** Page opened without `?id=`. Typing the address and leaving the field calls `saleAddressBlurLookup` (line 5642) -> `GET /api/buildings/search?q=` -> live Cotality building (verified: "100 Claremont Avenue" returns CLAREMONT HALL, ZIP 10027, 41 floors, TaxBlock 01992, TaxLot 0123) and fills the unit/building fields. Autosave (`autoSaveDraft`, 2-second debounce, line 8172) for a new listing only writes `localStorage.mallan_draft_sale`; the restore banner `_offerDraftRestore` (line 10146) is never called (restore only via `?restore=local`). **Save Draft** posts `collectSaleFormData()` (481 keys) to `POST /api/crm/listings`: the server (route.ts line 231) assigns `SL-NNNN` (advisory-locked MAX+1), sets `status='Draft'`, `agent_id` = session user, classifies `rls_eligible` (`lib/compliance/rls-eligibility.ts`: InHouse* or Commercial or unknown PropertyType = website-only; residential = eligible), and for eligible rows runs the REBNY validator and enforcement gate (422 today, section 0 item 1). It then stamps the Mallan listing-agent attribution from the authenticated agent into typed columns, dual-writes `listing_search_projection`, and returns `publicUrl`.

**Where data lands** (verified by psql after a full-form save): about 30 typed `listings` columns (list_price, bedrooms_total, bathrooms_full, living_area, borough, neighborhood, city, postal_code, property_type, property_sub_type, rls_eligible, idx_display_yn, internet_*_display_yn, commercial_*, auction_*, list_agent_*, list_office_name, listing_contract_date); `address` JSON (14 keys); `features` JSON (31-42 keys); **`raw_data` JSON = the entire payload** (479 keys incl. every `sale*` form key); `compliance` JSON (validation result); `media` JSON (empty); `listing_media` table (empty, upload 404). Every control therefore "lands" in raw_data even when it has no Cotality meaning; the register column "lands in" says which typed column/JSON also receives it.

**Edit / reopen.** `/crm/SALE-FORM-REDESIGN.html?id=<db id>` -> `_checkSaleEditMode` (line 9105) -> `GET /api/crm/listings/:id` -> `_populateSaleFormFromApi` (line 9722) restores controls from `SALE_FIELD_MAP`, `SALE_CHECKBOX_ARRAY_MAP`, `SALE_RADIO_MAP`. Controls not in those maps come back empty (section 8.1 column "reopen"). Autosave on edit sends the whole form to `PATCH /api/crm/listings/:id` 2 s after a change; Save Draft does the same. PATCH returns 200 even when `validation.valid=false` and the form says "Draft saved". Merging is `raw_data = existing U body`, so blank controls overwrite saved values (section 0 item 3).

**Publish.** Submit: (1) client checks `[required]` fields of all tabs and `validateREBNYRequired` (skipped for InHouse types) and the description compliance scan; (2) full PATCH; (3) PATCH `_crmWorkflowStatus`; (4) `PATCH /api/crm/listings/:id/status` with `CRM_TO_RESO_STATUS[status]` unless type is InHouseInternal; for Active it opens a panel with Public URL and RealPlus URL (RealPlus is null for website-only rows, so a toast + redirect is used). Verified: broker published SL-0029 Draft -> Active (first_active_date set, public page 200 with title "100 Claremont Avenue, #20B | $2,395,000") although the PATCH had returned 5 REBNY validation errors.

**Status machine (server, `status/route.ts:26`)**: Draft -> Active|ComingSoon; ComingSoon -> Active|Withdrawn; Active -> ActiveUnderContract|Pending|Hold|Withdrawn|Expired; ActiveUnderContract -> Active|Pending|Hold|Withdrawn; Pending -> Sold|Rented|Active|Withdrawn; Hold -> Active|Draft; Withdrawn|Expired -> Active|Draft; Sold, Rented, Cancelled terminal. Sold/Rented need BROKER and a ClosePrice. Observed 422s are in section 5.

**Ownership and who may edit** (`lib/auth/listing-capabilities.ts`): a row is *mallan-local* when `listing_id` starts SL-/RL- or `rls_eligible=false`. A local row may be read/edited/status-changed/uploaded-to by a BROKER or by the AGENT whose id equals `listings.agent_id` (verified: a different AGENT gets 403 on GET and PATCH). Rows synced from Cotality are *third-party-rls* (or *mallan-rls-return-copy*): never editable (PATCH -> 403 `SOURCE_OWNED_LISTING`, verified on id 83). Buyer/client sessions have no CRM write access (403) but still receive and render the form (section 0 item 10). The form has no read-only mode: a third-party listing opens with all inputs enabled and a DRAFT-style badge.

**Third-party listings are read-only data:** Cotality fields never flow back; `modification_timestamp` of synced rows must not be bumped (cursor safety), which is why PATCH is refused.

## 2. LINE-BY-LINE REGISTER: how to read it, and the Cotality contract it was verified against

Live proof used for field names (all saved under `scratchpad\form1-owner\`):
- `entity-fields.json` / `metadata.xml`: live `GET /odata/$metadata` (HTTP 200): Property 758 fields, Media 56, Member 91, Office 80, OpenHouse 47.
- `live-proof-select-names.json`: live `GET /odata/Property?$top=1&$select=<118 names>` -> 200, and each of 25 names the form emits or its validator requires -> **400**: auction_yn, auction_type, auction_start_date, auction_end_date, auction_terms_url, BathroomsTotal, CoBrokeAgreement, BuildingPetsAllowed, AttendanceType, BuildingLaundryFeatures, FlipTax, SponsorUnitYN, TaxAbatementYN, TaxAbatementComments, BuildingTaxLot, ElevatorsTotal, IDXEntireListingDisplayYN, NewDevelopmentYN, SyndicateYN, UnParsedAddress, MaximumFinancingPercent, MaximumFinancingRemarks, PercentOfCommonElements, TaxMonthlyAmount, FirstShowingDate.
- `live-proof-lookups.json`, `enums.json`: live `GET /odata/Lookup?$filter=LookupName eq '<name>'` for 13 lookups (StandardStatus 11, MlsStatus 26, StructureType 23, ListingAgreement 11, PetsAllowed 31, Flooring 63, View 85, MediaCategory 18, PropertyCondition 27, SpecialListingConditions 35, CommonInterest 13, PropertySubType 76, PropertyType 13). The distinct Lookup values equal the `$metadata` EnumType members exactly for all 13, so enums for all other pick lists come from `$metadata` (183 EnumTypes).
- `live-proof-listing.json`: live Property ListingKey 1193201950 / ListingId RLS20121202 (100 Claremont Avenue #20B, Claremont Hall), filter `StandardStatus eq 'Active' and PropertyType eq 'Residential' and PhotosCount gt 5 and VideosCount gt 0 and VirtualTourURLUnbranded ne null`.

Register columns: `#` DOM order; `src line` first line in the HTML where the id/name appears; `tab` main/sub tab (BLDG-MODAL, MEDIA-MODAL = modals); `sale?` Y yes / C conditional (houses only) / N not for a residential sale; `payload key emitted` = key in the real POST body (id, else name; `BOOLEAN` = checkbox group without ids where the generic collector keeps only the last box's true/false; `[]` = array); `Cotality Property field` = the field it must map to (all names were checked against `$metadata`; "(attr)" = the HTML's `data-rls-field` is a valid Property field but the payload does not use it); `lands in` = where the value was found in the row after a real save (`raw_data`, `features`, `address`, `col:<typed column>`; `a->b` = a canonical key derived from this control); `reopen` = kept / LOST / CHANGED / n/a after reopening the saved row with every control filled (some LOST items are cleared by field-visibility rules after populate; the acceptance script re-checks with a realistic fill); `verdict`; `defect`; `exact fix`.

## 3. MEDIA, VIDEO AND VIRTUAL-TOUR CONTROLS

### 3.1 What live Cotality actually carries (live proof)

- `Media` resource: `MediaCategory` enum has 18 members (Addendum, AerialView, AgentPhoto, BrandedVirtualTour, Disclosure, Document, FloorPlan, Map, OfficeLogo, OfficePhoto, Other, Photo, RentalDocuments, Restriction, Survey, Topography, UnbrandedVirtualTour, Video). **But the live account returns rows only for Photo and FloorPlan**: `GET /odata/Media?$filter=MediaCategory eq 'Video'` (and BrandedVirtualTour, UnbrandedVirtualTour, Document) -> 200 with **0 rows** (`live-proof-media-categories.json`). For the sample listing 1193201950: PhotosCount 21 = 20 Photo rows + 1 FloorPlan row (FloorPlansCount is null, so PhotosCount includes the floor plan row), `Order` 1..21, `PreferredPhotoYN` null on all, `ImageOf` null, MediaURL = `https://api.cotality.com/trestle/Media/Property/PHOTO-Jpeg/...`.
- Videos and tours arrive **as URLs on Property**, not as Media rows: `VirtualTourURLBranded` (+2,+3) and `VirtualTourURLUnbranded` (+2,+3). Live sample: VirtualTourURLUnbranded = `https://vimeo.com/895961785` (a video), another listing `https://youtu.be/dYKEgWP0WL8...`; of 200 newest Active listings VideosCount>0 on 15, VirtualTourURLUnbranded populated on 31, Branded on 9, `...2/...3` on 0 (`live-proof-media-tour-fields.json`). `VideosCount` can be 1 while no URL is present (ListingKey 1192617124: VideosCount 1, both URLs null). `DocumentsCount` (170/200) counts documents that are not retrievable as Media rows.
- Property-level media fields (live `$metadata`): PhotosCount, PhotosChangeTimestamp (timestamp), FloorPlansCount, TotalFloorPlansCount, FloorPlansChangeTimestamp, VideosCount, VideosChangeTimestamp, DocumentsCount, DocumentsAvailable (enum), DocumentsChangeTimestamp, VirtualTourURLBranded/Unbranded (+2,+3). Media entity (live): MediaKey, MediaCategory, MediaType (file format Jpeg/Pdf), MediaURL, Order, PreferredPhotoYN, ImageOf, ShortDescription, LongDescription, MediaStatus, ResourceRecordKey, X_MediaStream, Permission.
- Rule used by Mallan public DTO (`lib/media/listing-media-resolver.ts:892 splitTourUrls`): the public `videoUrl` and `virtualTourURL` are split **by host** (YouTube/Vimeo -> video; Matterport/3D -> tour), unbranded preferred over branded. `dossiers/media-contract.md` (shared media agent) had not been published when this was written; adopt it when it appears. Do not invent a second design: the existing routes `media/upload`, `media-order`, `media/[mediaId]`, `listing_media` table are the storage.

### 3.3 End-to-end result for the media fields (psql + public API, listing SL-0029 / id 91)

| step | observed |
|---|---|
| fill only Video URL (youtube), Video Tour URL (vimeo), Branded Tour (matterport); Save | raw_data keeps the three `sale*` keys; `VirtualTourURLUnbranded` empty; `/api/listings` `videoUrl` null, no tour (case A in `t14-media-public.json`) |
| Unbranded Tour = youtube.com/watch?v=5OLc_TDa5ss; Save | `raw_data.VirtualTourURLUnbranded` = same; `/api/listings` `videoUrl` = that URL (case B) |
| Unbranded Tour = my.matterport.com/show/?m=...; Save | `/api/listings` `virtualTourURL` = that URL; `videoUrl` null (case C) |
| upload a 1280x960 PNG as photo and as floor plan | 404 x2, toasts "Upload failed for ...: 404", `listing_media` 0 rows |
| reopen | the four non-Unbranded tour controls are restored from raw_data (SALE_FIELD_MAP); photo tiles come from `GET /media` (`renderServerMediaRows`, empty) |
| public page shown after Active | viewer props `images: []`, `floorPlanUrl: null`, `videoUrl` per host split, `virtualTourUrl` per host split |

Cached note: `/api/listings` is cached (in-memory + `s-maxage=60`); after an edit the old tour value is served for up to a minute. Append a unique query string when checking.

## 4. EVERY PICKLIST vs LIVE COTALITY (keep / remap / delete)

Source of allowed values: live `$metadata` EnumType members (identical to live `Lookup` rows for the 13 lookups tested; see section 2). Table rows list only options that do **not** match exactly; every other option of that control is KEEP (full per-option verdicts are in the register, column "verdict").

| form control (enum it must obey) | live enum size | option(s) that do not match exactly | action |
|---|---|---|---|
| `saleListingType` (ListingAgreement) | 11 | CoExclusive => CoExclusiveAgency; InHouseInternal => (no member); InHouseWebOnly => (no member); OwnerOptOut => (no member); ParticipantOnly => (no member) | REMAP CoExclusive -> CoExclusiveAgency; MOVE InHouseInternal to the distribution-mode control (4.3); MOVE InHouseWebOnly to the distribution-mode control (4.3); MOVE OwnerOptOut to the distribution-mode control (4.3); MOVE ParticipantOnly to the distribution-mode control (4.3); the other 3 KEEP |

| `saleCommonInterest` (CommonInterest) | 13 | none: all 8 options are exact live members | KEEP all |
| `saleBusinessType` (BusinessType) | 139 | none: all 21 options are exact live members | KEEP all |
| `saleLivingAreaUnits` (AreaUnits) | 3 | none: all 2 options are exact live members | KEEP all |
| `saleLivingAreaSource` (AreaSource) | 18 | none: all 9 options are exact live members | KEEP all |
| `saleConcessions` (Concessions) | 3 | none: all 3 options are exact live members | KEEP all |

| `saleCondition` (PropertyCondition) | 27 | Good => GoodCondition; Fair => (no member); Poor => PoorCondition; Updated => UpdatedRemodeled | REMAP Good -> GoodCondition; DELETE Fair (or Mallan-only tag); REMAP Poor -> PoorCondition; REMAP Updated -> UpdatedRemodeled; the other 4 KEEP |

## 5. STATUS HANDLING

Live Cotality `StandardStatus` (11): Active, ActiveUnderContract, Canceled, Closed, ComingSoon, Delete, Expired, Hold, Incomplete, Pending, Withdrawn. `MlsStatus` (26) adds ActiveOptionContract, AttorneyReview, CanceledRelisted, CompSold, Contingent, FirstLook, Leased, OptionPeriod, PendingBackupsRequested, PendingFeasibility, PendingInspection, PendingShortSale, PrepNoShow, PrepShow, Terminated.

What the form emits per option (run in the page, `collectSaleFormData().MlsStatus`) and what the server then accepts (`PATCH /listings/:id/status`, probed on id 95 with the broker session; full output `t13-status-matrix.txt`):

| form option | emitted MlsStatus | valid StandardStatus? | server transition result / action |
|---|---|---|---|
| Draft, Future | Draft | **no** | Draft = Mallan-only unpublished state. Never send. |
| ComingSoon | ComingSoon | yes | Draft->ComingSoon allowed. KEEP |
| Active, BackOnMarket | Active | yes | Draft->Active 200. KEEP (BackOnMarket = Mallan detail) |
| OfferOut, OfferThruUs, OfferAccepted, OAThruUs, ContractOut, COThruUs | ActiveUnderContract | yes | Pending->ActiveUnderContract is **422** (allowed: Sold, Rented, Active, Withdrawn); Active->ActiveUnderContract allowed. Keep as Mallan workflow detail (`_crmWorkflowStatus`), canonical = ActiveUnderContract |
| ContractSigned, ContractSignedThruUs, BoardApproved | Pending | yes | Active->Pending 200 |
| Sold, SoldThruUs | **Sold** | **no** (Cotality = Closed) | Active->Sold **422**; server only allows Pending->Sold. REMAP to Closed |
| Withdrawn, PermOffMarket | Withdrawn | yes | Active->Withdrawn 200; Withdrawn->Active/Draft only |
| Cancelled | **Cancelled** | **no** (Cotality = Canceled) | server has no Canceled; Active->Canceled **422**, Active->Cancelled **422** (not in Active's list). REMAP |
| TempOffMarket, Hold | Hold | yes | Active->Hold allowed |
| Expired | Expired | yes | Active->Expired allowed |
| (RLS System) Pending | Active (fallback) | emitted wrong | `getResoMlsStatus` returns `'Active'` for any unknown option (line 8590). REMAP to Pending |
| (RLS System) Closed | Active (fallback) | | server Active->Closed **422**. REMAP: server must accept Closed |
| (RLS System) Canceled | Active (fallback) | | server rejects Canceled **422** |
| (RLS System) Incomplete | Active (fallback) | | Incomplete = Cotality state for a listing missing data; not a user choice. DELETE the option |
| Delete | (not offered) | yes | never offer |

Three vocabularies exist and must become one: (1) form select values (26), (2) client `STATUS_TRANSITIONS` (line 8548) and `CRM_TO_RESO_STATUS` (line 8576), (3) server `STATUS_TRANSITIONS` (status/route.ts:26) with Sold/Rented/Cancelled. Target: server canonical statuses = Cotality StandardStatus plus the Mallan-only `Draft`; Sold -> Closed, Cancelled -> Canceled, Rented removed from the sale form; keep the form's richer workflow (offer/contract steps) only as `_crmWorkflowStatus` detail.

**What "Draft" means for a Mallan-authored listing**: not yet published; exists only in Mallan; has no Cotality StandardStatus; must never appear on public surfaces or be sent to Cotality; becomes `ComingSoon` or `Active` on publish. **Observed defect**: Draft rows are publicly viewable. `GET /listing/new-york-ny/sl-0025` (status Draft, rls_eligible=false) returned 200 with a rendered page and `robots index, follow`; the acceptance check S4 asserts 404. (The shared media agent saw the same for RLS-eligible Drafts with `idx_display_yn=true`.)

Other status-related facts (from code, lines 8049-8056; the unreachable-target path was not run end to end, the 422 transitions in the table above were): Submit with a status whose target is not reachable (e.g. Sold from Active) PATCHes the data, then shows "Saved as Draft. Publish failed: Invalid status transition..." and stays in the old status; `Draft` is reachable from Hold, Withdrawn, Expired. `saleStatus` is `required` on Draft; `saleSoldPrice`, `saleOffMarketDate`, `saleContractSignedDate` show required stars at all times.

## 6. THE AGENT BLOCK (ListAgentMlsId, ListAgentKey, co-list agents)

Live shape (from a real Active listing, `live-proof-listing.json`): ListAgentKey `28520347`, ListAgentMlsId `TM113`, ListAgentFullName "Claremont Hall Sales Office", ListOfficeKey `5660709`, ListOfficeMlsId `6182`, ListOfficeName "Corcoran Sunshine Marketing Group". **Key and MlsId are different identifiers**: Key = system key = `Member.MemberKey` (foreign key), MlsId = `Member.MemberMlsId`. Another live listing with a co-list agent (`live-proof-member-search.json`, ListingKey 1193204929): ListAgentKey 25247496 / MlsId 36382; CoListAgentKey 25251408 / CoListAgentMlsId 67976 / CoListAgentFullName "Benjamin Pofcher" / CoListOfficeKey 6747898 / CoListOfficeMlsId 40105 / CoListOfficeName "Sothebys International Realty". Note the sample listing's ListAgentMlsId `TM113` does not exist in `Member` (query by MemberMlsId and MemberKeyNumeric returned 0 rows): team/sales-office accounts can be list agents without a Member row, so lookups must tolerate a miss.

Live `Member` (91 fields; `live-proof-member2.json` shows a real row): MemberKey, MemberKeyNumeric, MemberMlsId, MemberFullName/First/Last, MemberEmail, MemberDirectPhone, MemberMobilePhone, MemberPreferredPhone, MemberStateLicense, MemberStatus (Active), MemberType, MemberNationalAssociationId(not in this select), OfficeKey, OfficeMlsId, OfficeName. Search that worked live: `GET /odata/Member?$filter=MemberStatus eq 'Active' and startswith(MemberLastName,'Delany')` -> 200, 1 row. `Office` (80 fields): OfficeKey, OfficeMlsId, OfficeName, OfficeStatus; `Office?$filter=OfficeName eq 'Corcoran Group'` -> OfficeKey 6629370, OfficeMlsId 40102.

Property fields for the agent block (all valid in live `$metadata`): ListAgentKey, ListAgentMlsId, ListAgentFullName, ListAgentEmail, ListAgentDirectPhone, ListOfficeKey, ListOfficeMlsId, ListOfficeName, CoListAgentKey/MlsId/FullName/Email/DirectPhone (and CoListAgent2*, CoListAgent3*), CoListOfficeKey/MlsId/Name, BuyerAgentKey/MlsId, ListTeamName, ListTeamMlsId.

What Form 1 does today (all verified in the browser/DB):
- `/api/auth/me` returns `mlsId` (= `agents.trestle_mls_id`), `name`, `email`, `phone`, `companyKey` "mallan"; `initLoggedInAgent` (line 4839) writes them into **hidden** inputs `saleUpdatingAgent*`, which are outside `.flex-1`; payload `ListAgentMlsId`, `ListAgentFullName`, `ListAgentEmail`, `ListAgentDirectPhone` = "" (agent 101, mlsId 148848). The server back-fills name/email/phone/office from the session into typed columns (list_agent_full_name "Form1 Owner", list_office_name "Mallan Real Estate Inc.") but **list_agent_mls_id stays empty**. A PATCH then blanks it again (verified on the augmented row: 148848 -> NULL).
- `saleListingAgent` (hidden `<input>` behind a searchable picker) holds the **internal agent id** (101), not an MLS id or key; after reopen it holds the MLS id when one was saved. `saleListingCompany` = "mallan".
- No control or payload key for ListAgentKey, ListOfficeKey, ListOfficeMlsId (deliberately blank: the Mallan office MLS id is "not verified" per the code comment at line 7553). The Mallan office id must come from a live `Office` lookup before it is written.
- Pickers: `rebnyCompanyNames = {mallan}` and `rebnyAgents = {mallan: []}` (lines 5970-5973, comment "Loaded from API" but nothing loads them). Verified in the page: company dropdown lists only Mallan; agent list says "Select a company first"/empty; clicking "Add Co-List Agent" creates `coListCompanySearch1`, `coListCompany1`, `coListAgentSearch1`, `coListAgentHidden1` and nothing can be chosen. Co-list payload keys never map to CoListAgent*.
- Buyer-agent picker (`saleBuyerCompany/Agent`): same empty source; the server validator demands BuyerAgentMlsId at listing creation, which cannot exist before a buyer.
- Server also accepts a forged attribution: the previous audit's agent-2 POST with `ListAgentFullName` of another agent was stored (not re-tested; the server stamps blank-only so a payload that includes the field wins). Fix: always overwrite list agent identity from the session on create.

Required behavior (fix spec):
1. Listing agent = the session agent: `ListAgentMlsId` = `agents.trestle_mls_id`, `ListAgentKey` = the matching live `Member.MemberKey` (resolve once by `Member?$filter=MemberMlsId eq '<id>'`, store on the agent), `ListAgentFullName/Email/DirectPhone` from the agent profile, `ListOfficeName` "Mallan Real Estate Inc." and `ListOfficeKey/MlsId` from a verified Office lookup. If the agent has no `trestle_mls_id`, block publish with a clear message (the acceptance script reports this).
2. Co-list agents may be (a) Mallan agents (roster API) or (b) any Cotality Member: search `Member` by name/MlsId (status Active), keep `MemberKey`, `MemberMlsId`, name, email, phone, `OfficeKey/OfficeMlsId/OfficeName`; emit `CoListAgentKey/MlsId/FullName/Email/DirectPhone` and `CoListOfficeKey/MlsId/Name` (slots 2 and 3 for further co-listers). Never copy MlsId into Key.
3. Buyer agent: show only at Pending/Closed; `BuyerAgentMlsId/Key` only then.
4. `saleListingCompany/Agent` pickers read from those sources; remove `rebnyAgents`/`rebnyCompanyNames` constants.

## 7. ACCEPTANCE SCRIPT

File: `C:\Users\MAYAAL~1\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\C--Users-MayaAllan-mallan-ops\27fa35f1-5c66-4ecc-9cd4-d892fc9ad7ea\scratchpad\dossiers\form1-acceptance.js` (runnable, Playwright core from the mallan-nyc checkout, psql, live Cotality through `mallan-ops/tools/mallan-cotality-env.js`, ~8 throttled provider requests per run).

Run: `node form1-acceptance.js` (env: `TOKEN` broker session cookie value, `LISTING_TYPE=ExclusiveRightToSell|InHouseWebOnly`, `REAL_ID` live ListingId, `BASE` must be localhost). It: reads a real live Cotality condo (default ListingKey 1193201950 / RLS20121202) and the live `$metadata` enums; opens the form; types the real address and checks the form's own Cotality building lookup fills BuildingName/YearBuilt/StoriesTotal/TaxBlock/TaxLot/ZIP/borough/SubdivisionName exactly as live; fills every control; Save Draft (expects 201); psql read-back of typed columns/raw_data and a live `$select` of every emitted capitalized key; asserts picklist values against live enums; asserts a Draft is not public; closes and reopens (every control must come back); edits price/description, saves, checks nothing else is clobbered; reopens again; uploads a photo and floor plan; sets Active and Submits; checks the public page, `/api/listings` videoUrl/virtualTourURL and that no private field leaks; probes the Cotality status vocabulary; checks agent/buyer/third-party permissions. Output: PASS/FAIL lines and a results JSON (env OUT); exit code = failures.

Last runs on main (this revision): website-only path `TOKEN=form1own-token LISTING_TYPE=InHouseWebOnly` -> 26 PASS / 24 FAIL (dossiers folder: `form1-acceptance-last-run.json` and `.txt`); Cotality-eligible path `LISTING_TYPE=ExclusiveRightToSell` stops at S3 (422), exactly as section 0 item 1. All 24 failures are tagged EXPECTED-FAIL-TODAY and map to the fix list. The script is the definition of done: after the fixes all checks must PASS with `LISTING_TYPE=ExclusiveRightToSell`.

## 9. EVIDENCE INDEX (all under `C:\Users\MAYAAL~1\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\C--Users-MayaAllan-mallan-ops\27fa35f1-5c66-4ecc-9cd4-d892fc9ad7ea\scratchpad\`)

Live Cotality (role folder `form1-owner\`): `live-proof-lookups.json` (13 Lookup queries), `live-proof-listing.json` (Property 1193201950), `live-proof-media.json` (Media of that listing), `live-proof-media-categories.json` (Media by category + a VideosCount listing with no URL), `live-proof-member.json`, `live-proof-member2.json`, `live-proof-member-search.json` (Member/Office/co-list sample), `live-proof-select-names.json` ($select of 118 valid names -> 200, 25 invalid -> 400), `live-proof-lookup-all.json` (first 40 pages of Lookup, unstable paging, informational), `metadata.xml`, `entity-fields.json`, `enums.json`, plus `dossiers\form1-acceptance-results.json`. Provider requests used by this owner: 101 (counter `cot-count.txt`) plus 28 by four acceptance runs (8+4+8+8) = about 129 of 150; the app itself also called Cotality through `/api/buildings/search`.
Browser/DB: `inv2.json` (831-control inventory with option lists and line numbers), `t1u-InHouseWebOnly-post.json` (the 481-key payload), `t1u-land.json` (where each key landed), `t1u-reopen.json` (reopen diff), `t4-post.json`, `t6-full.json` (augmented RLS-eligible create), `t5-diff-89.txt`, `t5-diff-91.txt` (psql diff after reopen+edit+save), `t13-status-matrix.txt`, `t9-status-proptype.json`, `t14-media-public.json`, `rls-422.json` (full validator error list), `register.md`, `fns.json`, scripts `t*.js`, `build1.js`, `build2.js`, `curated.js`.
Fixtures created in the throwaway DB by this owner: agent 101 (BROKER, trestle_mls_id 148848 = a real live Member.MemberMlsId used only as test data), agent 102 (AGENT), sessions `form1own-token`, `form1agent-token`; listings SL-0025..SL-0031 (ids 87-95) and several acceptance listings (SL-94xx). None touch production.

Note on counts: a static read of the markup (dossiersstructure-form1-sale-redesign.md) lists 815 controls; the live DOM after load has 831 (the 16 extra are created by script at load). This dossier counts the live DOM.
